## Module Descriptions

- 英文：Automatically places and breaks Respawn Anchors to harm entities.
  中文：自动放置并破坏重生锚来伤害实体。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/AnchorAura.java:258
  用途：Module Descriptions
- 英文：Automatically prevents Anchor Aura by placing a slab on your head.
  中文：通过在你头上放置台阶来自动防止锚点光环。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/AntiAnchor.java:39
  用途：Module Descriptions
- 英文：Automatically prevents Auto Anvil by placing between you and the anvil.
  中文：通过在你和铁砧之间放置方块来自动防止自动铁砧。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/AntiAnvil.java:39
  用途：Module Descriptions
- 英文：Places string to prevent beds being placed on you.
  中文：放置线以防止床被放在你身上。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/AntiBed.java:60
  用途：Module Descriptions
- 英文：Tries to dodge arrows coming at you.
  中文：尝试闪避射向你的箭矢。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/ArrowDodge.java:103
  用途：Module Descriptions
- 英文：Swaps to a target slot when you attack.
  中文：攻击时切换到目标槽位。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/AttributeSwap.java:312
  用途：Module Descriptions
- 英文：Automatically places anvils above players to destroy helmets.
  中文：自动在玩家上方放置铁砧来摧毁头盔。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/AutoAnvil.java:100
  用途：Module Descriptions
- 英文：Automatically equips armor.
  中文：自动装备护甲。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/AutoArmor.java:91
  用途：Module Descriptions
- 英文：Automatically mine blocks next to someone's feet.
  中文：自动挖掘别人脚边的方块。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/AutoCity.java:138
  用途：Module Descriptions
- 英文：Automatically repairs your armor and tools in pvp.
  中文：在 PvP 中自动修复你的护甲和工具。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/AutoEXP.java:80
  用途：Module Descriptions
- 英文：Automatically disconnects you when certain requirements are met.
  中文：满足特定条件时自动断开连接。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/AutoLog.java:152
  用途：Module Descriptions

- 英文： /                 What to do when your target is blocking with a shield: /                 - Ignore:   Don't attack them if they are blocking /                 - Break:    Swap to an axe to disable the shield (Only if Auto Switch is enabled) /                 - None:     Attack them as normal /             
  中文： / 目标正在用盾牌格挡时的处理方式： / - 忽略：不攻击正在格挡的目标。 / - 破盾：切换到斧使盾牌失效，需要启用自动切换。 / - 无：照常攻击。 / 
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/KillAura.java:98
  用途：Module Descriptions

  用途：Module Descriptions

  用途：Module Descriptions

  用途：Module Descriptions

  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/SelfWeb.java:62

- 英文：    What to do when your target is blocking with a shield: /     - Ignore:   Don't attack them if they are blocking /     - Break:    Swap to an axe to disable the shield (Only if Auto Switch is enabled) /     - None:     Attack them as normal / 
  中文：目标使用盾牌格挡时的处理方式： / - 忽略：目标格挡时不攻击。 / - 破盾：切换斧头使盾牌失效（需启用自动切换）。 / - 无：照常攻击。 / 
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/systems/modules/combat/KillAura.java:1
  用途：Setting Descriptions

- 英文：The "axis" command (aka GoalAxis) will go to a axis, or diagonal axis, at this Y level.
  中文：axis 命令会前往指定 Y 高度的坐标轴或对角线。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:251
  用途：Other
- 英文：Fill in blocks behind you (stealth +100)
  中文：在身后填补方块以隐藏路径。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:252
  用途：Other
- 英文：Set to 1.0 to effectively disable this feature
  中文：设为 1.0 可使此功能基本失效。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:253；src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:343；src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:345
  用途：Other
- 英文：When GetToBlockProcess or MineProcess fails to calculate a path, instead of just giving up, mark the closest instance of that block as "unreachable" and go towards the next closest.
  中文：无法计算到目标方块的路径时，将最近的方块标为无法到达，并尝试下一个目标。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:254
  用途：Other
- 英文：This is just a tiebreaker to make it less likely to break blocks if it can avoid it.
  中文：路径代价相同时，优先选择无需破坏方块的路线。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:255
  用途：Other
- 英文：It doesn't actually take twenty ticks to place a block, this cost is so high because we want to generally conserve blocks which might be limited.
  中文：放置方块的代价设得较高，以节约数量可能有限的方块。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:256
  用途：Other
- 英文：Block reach distance
  中文：方块交互距离。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:257
  用途：Other
- 英文：Blocks that Baritone will attempt to avoid (Used in avoidance)
  中文：Baritone 尝试避开的方块，供避让功能使用。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:258
  用途：Other
- 英文：blocks that baritone shouldn't break, but can if it needs to.
  中文：应尽量避免破坏、但必要时可以破坏的方块。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:259
  用途：Other
- 英文：Blocks that Baritone is not allowed to break
  中文：禁止 Baritone 破坏的方块。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:260
  用途：Other
- 英文：Multiply the cost of breaking a block that's correct in the builder's schematic by this coefficient
  中文：在建筑蓝图中位置正确的方块，其破坏代价乘以此系数。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:261
  用途：Other
- 英文：Allow standing above a block while mining it, in BuilderProcess
  中文：建造时允许站在方块上方挖掘该方块。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:262
  用途：Other
- 英文：Distance to scan every tick for updates.
  中文：每个游戏刻扫描更新的距离。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:263
  用途：Other
- 英文：A list of blocks to be treated as if they're air.
  中文：视为空气的方块列表。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:264
  用途：Other
- 英文：If this is true, the builder will ignore directionality of certain blocks like glazed terracotta.
  中文：建造时忽略部分方块的朝向，例如带釉陶瓦。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:265
  用途：Other
- 英文：If this is true, the builder will treat all non-air blocks as correct.
  中文：建造时将所有非空气方块视为正确。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:266
  用途：Other
- 英文：Don't consider the next layer in builder until the current one is done
  中文：完成当前层之后再处理下一层。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:267
  用途：Other
- 英文：Only build the selected part of schematics
  中文：仅建造蓝图中选中的部分。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:268
  用途：Other
- 英文：How far to move before repeating the build.
  中文：重复建造前移动的距离。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:269
  用途：Other
- 英文：How many times to buildrepeat.
  中文：重复建造的次数。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:270
  用途：Other
- 英文：Don't notify schematics that they are moved.
  中文：移动蓝图时不通知蓝图本身。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:271
  用途：Other
- 英文：A list of blocks to be treated as correct.
  中文：视为放置正确的方块列表。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:272
  用途：Other
- 英文：A mapping of blocks to blocks to be built instead
  中文：建造时使用的方块替换映射。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:273
  用途：Other
- 英文：A mapping of blocks to blocks treated as correct in their position.
  中文：在对应位置视为正确的方块替换映射。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:274
  用途：Other
- 英文：Cached chunks (regardless of if they're in RAM or saved to disk) expire and are deleted after this number of seconds -1 to disable
  中文：缓存区块在此秒数后过期并删除，包括内存和磁盘缓存；-1 表示禁用。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:275
  用途：Other
- 英文：0.0f = not visible, fully transparent (instead of setting this to 0, turn off renderCachedChunks) 1.0f = fully opaque
  中文：缓存区块的不透明度：0 为完全透明，1 为完全不透明；隐藏时建议关闭 renderCachedChunks。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:276
  用途：Other
- 英文：Cancel the current path if the goal has changed, and the path originally ended in the goal but doesn't anymore.
  中文：目标改变后，若原路径不再抵达目标，则取消当前路径。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:277
  用途：Other
- 英文：Censor coordinates in goals and block positions
  中文：隐藏目标和方块位置中的坐标。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:278
  用途：Other
- 英文：Censor arguments to ran commands, to hide, for example, coordinates to #goal
  中文：隐藏已执行命令的参数，例如 #goal 中的坐标。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:279
  用途：Other
- 英文：Allow chat based control of Baritone.
  中文：允许通过聊天命令控制 Baritone。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:280
  用途：Other
- 英文：Some clients like Impact try to force chatControl to off, so here's a second setting to do it anyway
  中文：备用聊天控制开关，用于部分客户端强制关闭 chatControl 的情况。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:281
  用途：Other
- 英文：Print all the debug messages to chat
  中文：将全部调试信息输出到聊天栏。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:282
  用途：Other
- 英文：The big one.
  中文：启用主要功能。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:283
  用途：Other

  中文：当前最佳候选路径的颜色。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:284
  用途：Other
- 英文：The color of the blocks to break
  中文：待破坏方块的颜色。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:285
  用途：Other

  中文：待放置方块的颜色。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:286
  用途：Other
- 英文：The color of the blocks to walk into
  中文：待进入方块的颜色。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:287
  用途：Other

  中文：当前路径的颜色。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:288
  用途：Other
- 英文：The color of the goal box
  中文：目标框的颜色。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:289
  用途：Other

  中文：目标框反转时的颜色。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:290
  用途：Other
- 英文：The color of the path to the most recent considered node
  中文：通向最近评估节点的路径颜色。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:291

  中文：下一段路径的颜色。

  中文：选区第一个角的颜色。

  中文：选区第二个角的颜色。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:295
  用途：Other
- 英文：For example, if you have Mining Fatigue or Haste, adjust the costs of breaking blocks accordingly.
  中文：根据挖掘疲劳或急迫等状态效果，调整破坏方块的代价。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:296
  用途：Other
- 英文：This is the big A* setting.
  中文：A* 寻路算法的主要设置。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:297
  用途：Other
- 英文：Stop 5 movements before anything that made the path COST_INF.
  中文：在使路径代价变为无穷大的动作之前，提前五个动作停止。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:298
  用途：Other
- 英文：After calculating a path (potentially through cached chunks), artificially cut it off to just the part that is entirely within currently loaded chunks.
  中文：计算路径后，将其截断为完全处于当前已加载区块内的部分。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:299
  用途：Other
- 英文：Desktop notifications
  中文：桌面通知。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:300
  用途：Other
- 英文：Turn this on if your exploration filter is enormous, you don't want it to check if it's done, and you are just fine with it just hanging on completion
  中文：探索范围极大时，不再检查是否已完成；结束时可能持续等待。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:301
  用途：Other
- 英文：Disconnect from the server upon arriving at your goal
  中文：抵达目标后断开服务器连接。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:302
  用途：Other
- 英文：Trim incorrect positions too far away, helps performance but hurts reliability in very large schematics
  中文：忽略过远的错误方块位置，提高性能，但可能降低大型蓝图的可靠性。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:303
  用途：Other
- 英文：Allows baritone to save bed waypoints when interacting with beds
  中文：与床交互时保存床的路径点。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:304
  用途：Other
- 英文：Allows baritone to save death waypoints
  中文：保存死亡位置路径点。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:305
  用途：Other
- 英文：Echo commands to chat when they are run
  中文：执行命令时将命令回显到聊天栏。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:306
  用途：Other
- 英文：When running a goto towards a nether portal block, walk all the way into the portal instead of stopping one block before.
  中文：前往下界传送门时，完全走入传送门，而不是停在门外一格。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:307
  用途：Other
- 英文：Take the 10 closest chunks, even if they aren't strictly tied for distance metric from origin.
  中文：选取最近的十个区块，即使它们与原点的距离并非严格相等。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:308
  用途：Other
- 英文：When GetToBlock or non-legit Mine doesn't know any locations for the desired block, explore randomly instead of giving up.
  中文：不知道目标方块的位置时，随机探索，而不是放弃。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:309
  用途：Other
- 英文：Attempt to maintain Y coordinate while exploring
  中文：探索时尽量保持当前 Y 高度。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:310
  用途：Other
- 英文：When the cache scan gives less blocks than the maximum threshold (but still above zero), scan the main world too.
  中文：缓存扫描找到的方块数量大于零但低于阈值时，同时扫描实际世界。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:311
  用途：Other
- 英文：Start fading out the path at 20 movements ahead, and stop rendering it entirely 30 movements ahead.
  中文：前方二十个动作处开始淡出路径，三十个动作以后完全隐藏。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:312
  用途：Other
- 英文：Pathing can never take longer than this, even if that means failing to find any path at all
  中文：寻路的最长耗时，超过后即使没找到路径也会停止。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:313
  用途：Other
- 英文：The actual GoalNear is set in this direction from the entity you're following.
  中文：跟随实体时，将实际附近目标设在实体的此方向。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:314
  用途：Other
- 英文：The actual GoalNear is set this distance away from the entity you're following
  中文：跟随实体时，将实际附近目标设在距离实体此距离的位置。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:315
  用途：Other
- 英文：The radius (for the GoalNear) of how close to your target position you actually have to be
  中文：到达目标位置所允许的距离半径。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:316
  用途：Other
- 英文：When mining block of a certain type, try to mine two at once instead of one.
  中文：挖掘指定类型的方块时，尝试同时挖掘两个，而不是一个。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:317
  用途：Other
- 英文：Move without having to force the client-sided rotations
  中文：移动时不强制改变客户端视角。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:318
  用途：Other
- 英文：As well as breaking from above, set a goal to up and to the side of all blocks to break.
  中文：除了从上方挖掘，也允许把待挖方块的上侧方设为目标。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:319
  用途：Other
- 英文：Line width of the goal when rendered, in pixels
  中文：目标框线条宽度，单位为像素。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:320；src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:398
  用途：Other
- 英文：The set of incorrect blocks can never grow beyond this size
  中文：错误方块位置集合的最大数量。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:321
  用途：Other
- 英文：Modification to the previous setting, only has effect if forceInternalMining is true If true, only apply the previous setting if the block adjacent to the goal isn't air.
  中文：仅在 forceInternalMining 启用且目标相邻方块不是空气时，应用上一项设置。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:322
  用途：Other
- 英文：Stop using tools just before they are going to break.
  中文：工具即将损坏时停止使用。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:323
  用途：Other
- 英文：Durability to leave on the tool when using itemSaver
  中文：启用工具保护时，为工具保留的耐久度。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:324
  用途：Other
- 英文：Additional penalty for hitting the space bar (ascend, pillar, or parkour) because it uses hunger
  中文：跳跃、垫高或跑酷会消耗饥饿值，因此额外增加按空格的代价。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:325
  用途：Other
- 英文：How high should the individual layers be?
  中文：每个建造层的高度。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:326
  用途：Other
- 英文：false = build from bottom to top
  中文：关闭时从底部向顶部建造。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:327
  用途：Other
- 英文：Disallow MineBehavior from using X-Ray to see where the ores are.
  中文：禁止挖矿功能使用透视查找矿石。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:328
  用途：Other
- 英文：Magically see ores that are separated diagonally from existing ores.
  中文：允许发现与已有矿石沿对角线相邻的矿石。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:329
  用途：Other
- 英文：What Y level to go to for legit strip mining
  中文：正常分支挖矿时使用的 Y 高度。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:330
  用途：Other
- 英文：Shows popup message in the upper right corner, similarly to when you make an advancement
  中文：在右上角显示类似进度提示的弹出消息。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:331
  用途：Other
- 英文：Build in map art mode, which makes baritone only care about the top block in each column
  中文：使用地图画建造模式，仅关注每列最上方的方块。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:332
  用途：Other
- 英文：After finding this many instances of the target block in the cache, it will stop expanding outward the chunk search.
  中文：缓存中找到此数量的目标方块后，停止扩大区块搜索范围。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:333
  用途：Other
- 英文：If a movement's cost increases by more than this amount between calculation and execution (due to changes in the environment / world), cancel and recalculate
  中文：执行动作时的代价比计算结果增加超过此值，则取消并重新寻路。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:334
  用途：Other
- 英文：How far are you allowed to fall onto solid ground (with a water bucket)? It's not that reliable, so I've set it below what would kill an unarmored player (23)
  中文：持有水桶时允许下落到地面的最大高度。落地水不完全可靠，默认值低于致命高度。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:335
  用途：Other
- 英文：How far are you allowed to fall onto solid ground (without a water bucket)? 3 won't deal any damage.
  中文：不持有水桶时允许下落到地面的最大高度；三格以内不会受伤。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:336
  用途：Other
- 英文：If we are more than 300 movements into the current path, discard the oldest segments, as they are no longer useful
  中文：沿当前路径前进超过三百个动作后，丢弃已经无用的旧路径段。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:337
  用途：Other
- 英文：While mining, wait this number of milliseconds after mining an ore to see if it will drop an item instead of immediately going onto the next one
  中文：挖完矿石后等待此毫秒数，以检查掉落物，再继续下一块。
  文件：src/main/java/meteordevelopment/meteorclient/pathing/BaritoneSettings.java:338
  用途：Other
- 英文：Rescan for the goal once every 5 ticks.
  中文：每五个游戏刻重新扫描目标。
